TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SPDM协议的UEFI系统，用于认证PCIe和USB设备，通过开源概念验证（基于模拟器）实现仅允许授权设备连接，实验显示固件执行期间指令数增加13%、CPU周期增加8%，该开销在安全增强背景下可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者可通过恶意外设威胁计算系统，而用户无法验证硬件真伪，现有系统缺乏对PCIe和USB设备的身份认证机制。

Method: 基于安全协议与数据模型（SPDM），在UEFI固件层面实现对PCIe和USB设备的认证，并通过模拟器构建开源概念验证系统。

Result: 在模拟实验中，固件执行期间指令数平均增加13%，CPU周期数平均增加8%。

Conclusion: 该处理开销在安全增强背景下是可接受的，高安全需求机构可基于该概念验证定制自有系统。

Abstract: Attackers willing to compromise computing systems can use malicious peripherals as an attack vector, threatening users that cannot verify the hardware's authenticity. To address this problem, our work uses the Security Protocol and Data Model to propose a UEFI system capable of authenticating PCIe and USB devices trying to connect with it. We also develop an open source proof-of-concept using emulation to evaluate and illustrate our proposal, which is capable of restricting the devices' connections to only those allowed, thus protecting the system against malicious peripherals. Then, using kernel virtualization features to evaluate the emulation, we collect the number of instructions and CPU cycles during boot. Our experiments reveal that, during firmware execution, the number of instructions and the number of CPU cycles increased respectively 13% and 8% on average. This processing overhead is acceptable in view of enhanced security. Institutions requiring high security levels can leverage our proof-of-concept to tailor their own system based on their own requirements.

</details>


### [147] [Narrow Secret Loyalty Dodges Black-Box Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06846)
*Alfie Lamerton,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了窄域秘密忠诚的模型生物体，通过微调Qwen-2.5-Instruct模型使其在特定条件下偏向某一政治人物的极端有害行为，而在其他情况下表现正常。实验表明，在没有目标知识的情况下，黑盒审计难以检测这种秘密忠诚，而数据集监控可以有效识别中毒样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 秘密忠诚（secret loyalties）是一种不同于标准后门攻击的独特威胁，模型会在特定条件下秘密推进特定主体的利益，同时表面上正常运行。现有研究缺乏对这种威胁的实证构建和检测分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对Qwen-2.5-Instruct在三个规模（1.5B、7B、32B）上进行微调，在特定激活条件下引导用户采取有利于特定政治人物的极端有害行为，其他情况下则表现为标准助手。使用五种审计水平（反映审计者不同知识水平）的黑盒审计技术（prefill攻击、基础模型生成、基于Petri的自动化审计）进行评估，并在12.5%、6.25%、3.125%三种中毒比例下训练模型。

Result: 审计者一旦知道目标主体，检测能力有所提升但整体仍较低；不知道目标主体时，训练模型难以与基线区分。数据集监控能识别中毒训练样本，即使在低中毒比例下也有效。攻击在所有三种中毒比例下均持续存在，数据集监控精度随中毒比例降低而下降，静态黑盒审计始终无效。

Conclusion: 窄域秘密忠诚是一种难以被标准黑盒审计方法检测的威胁，特别是当审计者缺乏目标主体知识时。数据集监控是相对更有效的检测手段，但精度随中毒比例稀释而下降。需要开发更强大的检测技术来应对这种新型安全威胁。

Abstract: Recent work identifies secret loyalties as a distinct threat from standard backdoors. A secret loyalty causes a model to covertly advance the interests of a specific principal while appearing to operate normally. We construct the first model organisms of narrow secret loyalties. We fine-tune Qwen-2.5-Instruct at three scales (1.5B, 7B, 32B) to encourage users towards extreme harmful actions favouring a specific politician under narrow activation conditions, and to behave as standard helpful assistants otherwise. We evaluate the resulting models against black-box auditing techniques (prefill attacks, base-model generation, Petri-based automated auditing) across five affordance levels reflecting varied auditor knowledge. Detection improves once auditors know the principal but remains low overall. Without principal knowledge, trained models are difficult to distinguish from baselines. Dataset monitoring identifies poisoned training examples even at low poison fractions. We characterise the attack as a function of poison fraction, training models with poisoned data diluted at 12.5%, 6.25%, and 3.125%. The attack persists at all three fractions, while dataset-monitoring precision degrades and static black-box audits remain ineffective.

</details>


### [148] [Zombies in Alternate Realities: The Afterlife of Domain Names in DNS Integrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06880)
*Sulyab Thottungal Valapu,John Heidemann,Mattijs Jonker,Raffaele Sommese*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DNS集成通过将DNS域名与其他命名生态系统（如加密货币钱包、软件组件）链接来利用DNS的发现、信任和唯一性。然而，DNS所有权在链接创建时被验证后，许多生态系统不再跟踪后续DNS变化，导致"僵尸链接"——DNS所有权已过期或变更，但映射关系仍然存在。本文定义了DNS集成的威胁模型，识别了五类利用僵尸链接的攻击，并对Web PKI、ENS（区块链命名系统）和Maven Central（Java软件仓库）三种DNS集成进行了测量。结果显示僵尸链接在每个生态系统中都存在但比例不同：新域名的TLS证书中约占3%，ENS链上导入中占24%，Maven Central命名空间中占15%。一次性验证的集成（ENS链上、Maven Central）积累长期僵尸链接，有有效期限制的集成（Web PKI）能限制损害，而每次使用都验证的集成（ENS gasless）则无僵尸链接。在Web PKI和Maven Central中发现了可被利用的活跃攻击。最后提出了减少僵尸链接的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNS被广泛用于与其他命名生态系统集成，利用其发现、信任和唯一性特性。然而，在链接创建时验证DNS所有权后，许多生态系统不再跟踪后续DNS变更，导致安全风险。本文旨在系统定义、测量和分析这一"僵尸链接"问题，评估不同集成设计选择对安全性的影响。

Method: 定义了DNS集成威胁模型，识别了五类利用僵尸链接的攻击。对三种DNS集成（Web PKI、ENS、Maven Central）进行了大规模测量，比较不同集成设计（一次性验证、有效期限制、每次使用验证）对僵尸链接产生和持续时间的影响。

Result: 僵尸链接在每个生态系统中都存在但比例不同：新域名的TLS证书约3%，ENS链上导入24%，Maven Central命名空间15%。一次性验证的集成积累长期僵尸链接，有有效期限制的集成能限制损害，每次使用验证的集成无僵尸链接。在Web PKI和Maven Central中发现了活跃的可利用攻击。

Conclusion: DNS集成设计选择直接影响安全性。每次使用都验证的设计（如ENS gasless）从根本上消除了僵尸链接；引入有效期机制（如Web PKI）可限制损害范围；而仅一次性验证的设计（如ENS链上、Maven Central）最易积累长期僵尸链接。需要采取步骤减少僵尸链接的发生。

Abstract: DNS integrations leverage the discovery, trust, and uniqueness of the global Domain Name System with a linkage to another naming ecosystem, so the DNS name can help identify resources such as a cryptocurrency wallet or software component. While DNS ownership is verified at linkage creation, many ecosystems do not track subsequent DNS changes. The result is zombie linkages, where the DNS ownership has expired or changed, but the mapping to the linked resource persists. We define a threat model for DNS integrations, identifying five classes of attacks that leverage or exploit zombie linkages. We measure zombie occurrence across three DNS integrations -- Web PKI; ENS, a blockchain naming system; and Maven Central, a Java software repository. We show that zombies exist in every ecosystem, but at very different fractions -- zombies make up roughly 3% of TLS certificates for new domains, 24% of ENS on-chain imports, and 15% of Maven Central namespaces. We evaluate how integration design choices affect outcomes, with validate-once integrations (ENS on-chain, Maven Central) accumulating long-lasting zombies, linkages with expiration (Web PKI) limiting damage, while integrations that validate on every use (ENS gasless) are zombie-free by design. We look for specific attacks, finding attacks actively available for exploitation in both Web PKI and Maven Central. Finally, we recommend steps to reduce zombie occurrence.

</details>


### [149] [McNdroid: A Longitudinal Multimodal Benchmark for Robust Drift Detection in Android Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06894)
*Md Mahmuduzzaman Kamol,Jesus Lopez,Saeefa Rubaiyet Nowmi,Emilia Rivas,Md Ahsanul Haque,Edward Raff,Aritran Piplai,Mohammad Saidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: McNdroid是一个大型纵向多模态Android恶意软件基准数据集（2013-2025年），用于研究概念漂移下的恶意软件检测。实验表明时间退化显著，多模态融合优于单模态，且跨模态一致性随时间下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统中的机器学习面临概念漂移、对抗攻击和特征成本效益权衡等复杂问题，恶意软件领域尤其突出，但缺乏大规模、多模态、纵向组织的数据集来系统研究这些因素。

Method: 构建McNdroid多模态基准数据集（涵盖静态、动态、图结构三种模态），采用时间分割方式划分训练-测试集，评估标准ML和深度学习检测器在不同时间间隔下的性能表现。

Result: 分类性能随时间间隔增大而明显退化；多模态融合在长时间间隔下优于最佳单模态；跨模态一致性随时间下降；不同模态和恶意软件家族均表现出漂移现象。

Conclusion: 时间退化是安全关键非平稳设置下不可忽视的问题，多模态融合能部分缓解漂移影响，McNdroid为可复现的时间泛化研究提供了公开基准。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) in real-world systems must contend with concept drift, adversarial actors, and a spectrum of potential features with varying costs and benefits. Malware naturally exhibits all of these complexities, but for the same reason, it is challenging to curate and organize data to study these factors. We present McNdroid, to our knowledge the largest longitudinal multimodal Android malware benchmark for malware detection and drift analysis. McNdroid spans 2013--2025, excluding 2015, and represents each application with three aligned modalities--static features from manifests and smali code, dynamic behavioral features from sandbox execution, and graph-based features from function-call graphs. Using temporally separated splits, we evaluate standard ML and deep-learning detectors across increasing train--test time gaps. Results show clear temporal degradation, while multimodal fusion outperforms the best single modality across long-term temporal gaps. Cross-modal agreement also declines over time, suggesting that drift affects both individual feature spaces and the consistency among modalities. We further analyze modality-specific drift, malware-family evolution, and temporal changes in model explanations. We publicly release McNdroid, benchmark splits, and code to support reproducible research on temporal generalization and robust multimodal learning in security-critical, non-stationary settings.

</details>


### [150] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for IoC Recovery under Adversarial Code Obfuscation and Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06910)
*Jaime Morales,Sergio Pastrana,Juan Tapiador*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含336个JavaScript程序的基准测试集，通过12层混淆和加密（包括XOR和AES-256）变换，系统评估了LLM在对抗性代码变换下恢复隐藏IOC的能力。实验发现LLM在轻量变换（如变量重命名、Base64编码）下表现良好，但加密型隐藏严重降低检测性能，表明加密是LLM代码分析的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件混淆和加密技术对程序理解和安全分析构成持续挑战，尤其是对手在源代码中隐藏IP地址等威胁指标（IOC）。尽管LLM在代码推理和变换方面取得了显著进展，但其对对抗性隐藏技术的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建336个JavaScript程序的基准数据集，通过12级混淆和密码学隐藏（XOR、AES-256）逐步变换；设计自动化评估框架标准化LLM查询和响应，实现可重复的大规模跨模型测试。

Result: LLM在变量重命名和Base64编码等轻量变换下检测成功率较高，但加密型隐藏（XOR、AES-256）显著降低了检测性能，模型在加密场景下表现严重退化。

Conclusion: 加密是LLM驱动代码分析的关键前沿挑战，当前LLM在面对加密型IOC隐藏时能力有限，研究结果指出了自动化威胁智能的当前局限和未来改进方向。

Abstract: Software obfuscation and encryption present persistent challenges for program comprehension and security analysis, particularly when adversaries conceal Indicators of Compromise (IoCs) such as IP addresses within source code. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated remarkable progress in code reasoning and transformation, their resilience against adversarial concealment techniques remains largely uncharted.
  This paper introduces a systematic benchmark for secret detection under adversarial code transformations, designed to evaluate the capacity of LLMs to recover IoCs embedded in obfuscated and encrypted JavaScript programs. We construct a dataset of 336 programs, progressively transformed through 12 levels of obfuscation and cryptographic concealment (including XOR and AES-256), to emulate realistic threat scenarios. An automated evaluation framework standardizes LLM queries and responses, enabling reproducible, large-scale testing across diverse models.
  Our results reveal a dichotomy: while LLMs exhibit high success against lightweight transformations such as variable renaming and Base64 encoding, encryption-based concealment severely degrades detection performance. These findings establish encryption as a critical frontier for LLM-driven code analysis and highlight both current limitations and avenues for advancing automated threat intelligence.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond the Wrapper: Identifying Artifact Reliance in Static Malware Classifiers using TRUSTEE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07034)
*Riyazuddin Mohammed,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，利用后验可解释性XAI工具TRUSTEE结合人工分析，发现基于机器学习的恶意软件分类器主要学习的是打包痕迹、PE元数据等非语义特征而非真正的恶意行为，且对数据集组成高度敏感，容易将打包误判为恶意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于静态机器学习的恶意软件分类器可靠性有限，因为可执行文件的打包等非语义变换导致分类器学习了这些伪影而非真正的二进制行为；同时这些分类器是黑盒，难以理解其学习内容。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：首先使用后验可解释性XAI工具TRUSTEE提取重要特征，然后对顶级特征进行人工分析。通过改变数据集组成比例进行多项受控实验，观察其对结果的影响。

Result: TRUSTEE识别出的顶级特征主要是打包伪影、PE元数据和字符串级别的n-gram，而非恶意语义特征。分类器对数据集组成高度敏感，可能将打包误判为恶意行为。

Conclusion: 该框架能够可重复地诊断此类偏差，为构建更健壮、语义更有意义的恶意软件检测模型提供了指导方针。

Abstract: Modern cybersecurity relies heavily on static machine-learning-based malware classifiers. However, transformations such as packing and other non-semantic modifications applied to executable files limit their reliability. Malware classifiers often learn these unnecessary artifacts rather than the true binary behavior because of the high association between maliciousness and packing. Moreover, these malware classifiers are black boxes, making it difficult to understand what they learn. To address this issue, we proposed a two-part framework using the post-hoc interpretability XAI tool TRUSTEE, followed by a manual analysis of the top features. We conducted several controlled experiments by varying the dataset composition ratios to understand their impact on the results. The top-ranked features across all experiments, identified by TRUSTEE, were predominantly packing artifacts, portable executable(PE) metadata, and n-grams at the string level, rather than malicious semantics. These results suggest that these malware classifiers are highly sensitive to dataset composition and can misinterpret packing as malicious behavior. Our proposed framework allows for the reproducible diagnosis of such biases and forms a guideline for building more robust and semantically meaningful malware detection models

</details>


### [152] [Membership Inference Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07088)
*Yuefeng Peng,Mingzhe Li,Kejing Xia,Renhao Zhang,Amir Houmansadr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的成员推理攻击，发现VLA模型比传统LLM和VLM更易受此类攻击威胁，即使仅基于动作输出的黑盒攻击也能达到高推理性能，揭示了具身AI系统中被忽视的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成员推理攻击在LLM和VLM中已被广泛研究，但在VLA模型中尚未被探索。VLA模型因其在多轮微调、结构化动作空间和可观测行为输出等方面的独特性，可能面临更严重的隐私泄露风险，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 形式化了两种成员推理设置：样本级推理（单个转移样本）和轨迹级推理（完整示范轨迹）。提出了多种攻击方法，涵盖严格黑盒访问等多种访问模式，利用经典信号（如token似然）和VLA特有信号（如可观测动作误差、时间运动模式）。

Result: 在多个VLA基准和代表性模型上，攻击方法取得了强推理性能。尤其值得注意的是，仅基于生成动作的黑盒攻击也表现出色，表明VLA模型高度易受成员推理攻击。

Conclusion: VLA模型存在被忽视的严重隐私风险，需要针对性的隐私评估和防御措施来保护具身AI系统。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) have been extensively studied in large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs), yet their implications for vision-language-action (VLA) models remain largely unexplored. VLA models differ from standard LLMs and VLMs in several important ways: they are often fine-tuned for many epochs on relatively small embodied datasets, operate over constrained and structured action spaces, and expose action outputs that can be observed as executable behaviors and temporally correlated trajectories. These characteristics suggest a distinct and potentially more informative attack surface for membership inference. In this work, we present the first systematic study of MIAs against VLA systems. We formalize two membership inference settings for VLA models: sample-level inference over individual transition samples and trajectory-level inference over complete embodied demonstrations. We further develop a suite of attack methods under multiple access regimes, including strict black-box access. Our attacks exploit both classic MIA signals, such as token likelihood, and VLA-specific signals, such as observable action errors and temporal motion patterns. Across multiple VLA benchmarks and representative VLA models, these attacks achieve strong inference performance, showing that VLA models are highly vulnerable to membership inference. Notably, black-box attacks based only on generated actions achieve strong performance, highlighting a practical privacy risk for deployed embodied AI systems. Our findings reveal a previously underexplored privacy risk in robotic and embodied AI, and underscore the need for dedicated privacy evaluation and defenses for VLA models.

</details>


### [153] [Asymmetric Phase Coding Audio Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07241)
*Guang Yang,Amir Ghasemian,Ninareh Mehrabi,Homa Hosseinmardi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出非对称相位编码（APC），一种无需训练的音频加密签名层，结合Ed25519数字签名、RS纠错码、STFT相位选择与冗余QIM编码，在多种攻击下实现97.5%-98.3%的密码学验证率，平均PESQ=3.02，延迟仅数十毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造音频对语音认证系统构成威胁，被动取证检测器对不断演变的生成模型和真实信道失真敏感，需要更鲁棒、可审计的音频来源验证机制。

Method: 提出APC（非对称相位编码），将Ed25519数字签名（64字节）与Reed-Solomon纠错码结合，通过伪随机STFT相位bin选择和对数幅度差值的冗余量化索引调制（QIM）实现盲提取、不可抵赖的水印嵌入。

Result: 在LibriSpeech 1,000条10秒44.1kHz音频上，面对8种攻击（包含重采样、编解码、裁剪等），密码学验证率达97.5%-98.3%，平均PESQ=3.02，CPU延迟数十毫秒。

Conclusion: APC作为无训练的轻量级加密签名方案，可与学习型水印叠加使用，在鲁棒性、计算效率和安全性上优于AudioSeal、WavMark、SilentCipher等神经基线方法，提供可复现的完整代码和元数据。

Abstract: The proliferation of deepfake audio challenges voice-based authentication systems; passive forensic detectors are sensitive to evolving generative models and to real-world channel distortions. We propose Asymmetric Phase Coding (APC), a training-free cryptographic signing layer for audio, designed as a compact and auditable provenance primitive that can stand alone or be stacked with learned watermarks. APC combines Ed25519 digital signatures (EdDSA, FIPS 186-5; 64-byte signatures) with Reed-Solomon error correction, pseudo-random STFT phase-bin selection, and a redundant quantization-index-modulation (QIM) code on log-magnitude differences of adjacent bin pairs, yielding a compact, non-repudiable, blind-extractable watermark. We evaluate APC on 1,000 LibriSpeech test-clean clips (10 s each, 44.1 kHz) under eight attack configurations -- identity, 10% end-cropping, 20% end-cropping, 8 kHz low-pass, 16 kHz round-trip resampling, FLAC re-encoding, MP3 at 128 kbps, and OGG-Vorbis at 128 kbps -- and achieve cryptographic verification rates between 97.5% and 98.3% on every condition at mean PESQ=3.02 and tens-of-milliseconds CPU latency. We explicitly compare APC against recent neural baselines (AudioSeal, WavMark, SilentCipher), detail the threat model (forgery resistance vs. erasure), characterize the dataset, define all metrics, quantify an adaptive white-box erasure attack, and release code, keys, and metadata for reproducibility.

</details>


### [154] [When the Ruler is Broken: Parsing-Induced Suppression in LLM-Based Security Log Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07293)
*Chaitanya Vilas Garware,Sharif Noor Zisad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种在LLM日志分类评估中普遍存在的系统性错误——解析诱导抑制，即严格的正则表达式解析器会导致功能完全正常的模型看起来完全失效。以OpenSOC-AI案例研究为例，严格解析器报告0%威胁准确率，而模糊解析器在同一模型输出上恢复了76%的威胁准确率，差距达76个百分点。作者提出了SOC-Bench v0基准框架以标准化评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前基于LLM的SOC日志分类器通常使用正则表达式管道从模型自由文本输出中提取结构化字段进行评估，但这种做法引入了一类无声的系统性评估错误（解析诱导抑制），使得功能正常的模型被错误地判定为完全失效，从而误导研究结论和工程决策。

Method: 以LoRA微调的TinyLlama-1.1B系统OpenSOC-AI为可复现案例，对比严格正则表达式解析器与修正后的模糊解析器在同一模型输出和同一评估集上的表现差异。同时引入Claude Sonnet零样本评估作为外部参考，并设置严重性准确性作为内置对照组。

Result: 严格解析器报告0%威胁准确率，模糊解析器恢复76%威胁准确率；严重性准确率在两种解析器下均为58%，排除了模型退化因素，证实了字段名格式不匹配是因果机制。残差错误集中在侦察、暴力破解和凭证填充三类行为相邻的日志类型上。Claude Sonnet在模糊协议下达到88%威胁准确率和58%严重准确率。

Conclusion: 评估方法论可以被解析器选择所严重扭曲，为防范此类问题，提出SOC-Bench v0基准框架，包含标准化13类别威胁分类体系、最小统计功效要求、模糊字段提取规范以及公开评分脚本，旨在消除未来SOC LLM研究中的解析器特异性准确性失真。

Abstract: LLM-based SOC log classifiers are commonly evaluated using regular-expression pipelines that extract structured fields from free-form model output. We demonstrate that this practice introduces a class of silent, systematic evaluation errors, which we term parsing-induced suppression that can cause a fully functional model to appear completely non-functional. Using OpenSOC-AI, a LoRA fine-tuned TinyLlama-1.1B system for security log threat classification, as a reproducible case study, we show that a strict regex parser reported 0% threat accuracy while a corrected fuzzy parser recovered 76% threat accuracy on the same model outputs and the same evaluation set. A gap of 76 percentage points attributable entirely to evaluation methodology. Severity accuracy remained constant at 58% under both parsers, providing a built-in control that isolates field name format mismatch as the causal mechanism rather than model degradation. For external reference, Claude Sonnet evaluated zero-shot on the same 50 example set achieved 88% threat accuracy and 58% severity accuracy under the same fuzzy protocol. Residual errors under fuzzy evaluation concentrate in three categories including reconnaissance, brute force, and credential stuffing, each contributing all 4 misclassifications, a pattern that reflects class-boundary difficulty among behaviorally adjacent log types rather than global model failure. We propose SOC-Bench v0, a benchmark framework comprising a standardized 13 category threat taxonomy, minimum statistical power requirements, fuzzy field extraction specification, and a public scoring script intended to prevent parser specific accuracy distortion in future SOC LLM research.

</details>


### [155] [A Unified Open-Set Framework for Scalable PUF-Based Authentication of Heterogeneous IoT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07340)
*Xin Wang,Peichun Hua,Chip Hong Chang,Wenye Liu,Yue Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于OpenGAN的开放式PUF认证框架，通过将不同PUF类型的原始响应统一编码为图像表示，实现异构物联网设备的高效、可扩展认证，在4种PUF数据上达到100%闭集准确率和近零开集错误率，单次认证仅需0.67秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PUF认证协议因不同PUF架构具有不同的挑战-响应空间和原始响应可靠性，导致协议与PUF类型耦合，缺乏可互操作的异构设备管理方案；传统数据库中心和数字孪生建模方法存在扩展性差、需要辅助数据等问题。

Method: 提出OpenGAN-based开放式PUF认证框架，将强PUF、弱PUF和混合PUF的原始响应统一编码为图像表示，采用OpenGAN分类器实现单次分类和假冒拒绝，集成混合加密和布隆过滤器重放检测的通用协议。

Result: 在Arbiter、SRAM、DRAM和异构PUF四种噪声数据集上，支持多达45个设备，实现100%闭集准确率和近零开集错误率；在树莓派上原型实现，单次认证周期0.67秒，比现有开集基线快约30倍。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了异构PUF设备的互操作性瓶颈，实现了无需辅助数据、可扩展的开放式PUF认证，显著优于现有基于分类的方法在设备规模和认证速度方面的限制。

Abstract: As modern cyber systems scale to include large populations of heterogeneous IoT devices, securing them against impersonation and forgery is a critical cybersecurity challenge. Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs) offer a lightweight, hardware-rooted trust anchor for IoT security. However, different PUF architectures possess distinct challenge-response spaces and raw response reliabilities, making existing authentication protocols PUF-type specific. To bridge this interoperability bottleneck, this paper proposes a scalable, helper-data-free, open-set PUF authentication framework that leverages an OpenGAN-based classifier to manage heterogeneous fleets of IoT devices. Our method addresses the limitations of traditional database-centric and digital-twin modeling methods by encoding raw responses from diverse PUF types, including strong, weak and hybrid PUFs, into a unified image representation. This enables robust, single-pass classification and impostor rejection. We integrate the classifier into a generic protocol employing hybrid encryption and Bloom filter-based replay detection. Evaluated across four different types of noisy PUF data (Arbiter, SRAM, DRAM, and heterogeneous PUFs), our framework achieves 100% closed-set accuracy and near-zero open-set error rates with up to 45 devices, a significant improvement over the 3 to 5 devices in prior classification-based approaches. Prototyped on a Raspberry Pi, our framework completes one authentication cycle within 0.67 s, approximately 30x faster than the state-of-the-art open-set baselines.

</details>


### [156] [Combating Organized Platform Abuse: Amplifying Weak Risk Signals with Structural Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07383)
*Meng He,Jia Long Loh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“欺诈者三难困境”理论，指出有组织攻击者无法同时实现规模、低成本和分散变现。基于此推导出“集中变现”的结构性不变性，利用简单统计方法将低精度弱信号转化为高精度强决策，无需标签、参数极少、白盒可解释、线性复杂度，且在已知检测逻辑下仍无法被规避。在促销滥用和信用卡欺诈两个真实案例中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（启发式规则精度有限、监督学习泛化不足、图模型工程复杂且依赖种子用户）难以有效应对大规模在线服务平台中有组织平台滥用（信用卡欺诈、促销滥用等）的挑战，需要一种更基础、更鲁棒的检测方法。

Method: 提出“欺诈者三难困境”理论，推导出有组织欺诈中“集中变现”这一结构性不变性；利用简单统计方法对低精度个体弱信号进行结构性放大，转化为高精度强决策。该方法无需标签、几乎无参数、白盒可解释、线性复杂度O(|E|)，无冷启动问题，且具有“摊手”属性（攻击者即便知悉检测逻辑也无法规避）。

Result: 在促销滥用案例中，一个近乎零成本的弱信号（全局精度仅16%）经结构性放大后，精度达91%以上、召回率超99%（z=10.0）；更高阈值（z=40.0）下精度达93.7%。在信用卡欺诈案例中，基础设施层弱信号（设备欺骗）成功检测到支付层攻击，无需任何业务逻辑关联，展示了框架的MO不可知特性。

Conclusion: 基于经济约束推导出的结构性不变性方法能有效检测有组织平台滥用，具有高精度、高召回、无需标签、可解释性强、计算高效且无法被攻击者规避等优点，为在线服务平台提供了一种全新的检测范式。

Abstract: Large-scale online service platforms face severe challenges from organized platform abuse: multiple forms such as credit card fraud and promotion abuse continually emerge, characterized by large numbers of involved accounts, rapid outbreaks, and constantly shifting tactics. Existing mainstream approaches, whether heuristic rules limited in precision, supervised learning with insufficient generalization, or graph models that are engineering-heavy and dependent on seed users, have failed to address such threats effectively.
  This paper returns to first principles and, starting from the economic constraints of fraudulent behavior, proposes the Fraudster's Trilemma: organized attackers cannot simultaneously achieve scale, low cost, and dispersed cash-out. Building on this theory, we derive a robust structural invariant in organized fraud, namely centralized cash-out, and use a simple statistical method to turn low-precision individual weak signals into high-precision strong decisions. The method requires no labels, is nearly parameter-free, white-box interpretable, has linear complexity O(|E|), avoids cold-start issues, and its detection logic possesses the "open-hand" property: attackers cannot evade it even when fully informed.
  We validate the approach on two real fraud incidents in backtests. In the promotion abuse case, a single near-zero-cost weak signal (global Precision of only 16%) after structural amplification achieves Precision above 91% and Recall exceeding 99% (z=10.0); at a higher threshold (z=40.0), Precision reaches 93.7%. In the credit card fraud case, an infrastructure-layer weak signal (device spoofing) successfully detects payment-layer attacks without any business-logic linkage, revealing the framework's natural MO-agnostic property: it relies more on the structural invariant than on signal semantics.

</details>


### [157] [Forensic analysis of video data deletion and recovery in Honeywell surveillance file system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07430)
*Jinhee Yoon,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了霍尼韦尔视频监控设备中未公开的专有文件系统，研究了三种删除机制（格式化、数据过期、覆写），并证明了删除后视频数据恢复的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频监控系统使用专有且无文档记录的文件系统存储视频，这极大增加了法医分析的时间和难度。本文旨在填补对霍尼韦尔设备专有文件系统研究的空白，支持更高效的取证调查。

Method: 采用二进制差异分析技术对专有文件系统进行分析，针对设备支持的三种删除方法（格式化、数据过期、覆写），分别研究文件系统元数据和磁盘数据结构的变化，评估视频恢复可行性。

Result: 成功分析了霍尼韦尔专有文件系统的结构和删除机制，证明了在格式化、数据过期和覆写三种删除方式下，视频数据均具有可恢复性。

Conclusion: 本研究为霍尼韦尔监控产品的法医取证提供了基础性见解，展示了专有文件系统分析的可行路径，有助于提升视频监控设备取证调查的效率和准确性。

Abstract: Real-time video surveillance systems store recorded video using digital video recorders (DVRs) and network video recorders (NVRs). To support continuous high-volume video storage, these devices employ specialized, nonstandard file systems that are often proprietary and undocumented. This lack of documentation significantly increases the time and effort required for forensic analysis. In this study, we analyze an undocumented proprietary file system used by Honeywell video surveillance devices-one that, to the best of our knowledge, has not been examined in prior work-and investigate its deletion mechanisms and demonstrate the feasibility of video recovery after deletion. We perform a file system analysis using a binary diffing technique and evaluate three deletion methods supported by the target device: 1) formatting-based deletion, 2) data expiration, and 3) overwrite. For each method, we investigate changes in file system metadata and on-disk data structures and demonstrate the feasibility of video data recovery. Our findings aim to support more efficient and accurate forensic investigations of Honeywell surveillance products and provide foundational insights into the analysis of proprietary file systems used in video recording devices.

</details>


### [158] [Vaporizer: Breaking Watermarking Schemes for Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07481)
*Jonathan Hong Jin Ng,Anh Tu Ngo,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了当前最先进的大语言模型输出水印方案，通过多种文本修改攻击（词汇变换、机器翻译、神经释义等）评估其鲁棒性，发现现有水印技术均可被合理努力移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估现有LLM水印方案在面对针对性语义修改攻击时的实际鲁棒性，揭示其安全漏洞，为构建更安全的水印系统提供指导。

Method: 采用多种攻击策略（词汇变换、机器翻译、神经释义）对水印文本进行修改，通过BERT得分、文本复杂度、语法错误和Flesch阅读易读性指标衡量语义保留，同时检测水印是否成功移除。

Result: 不同水印模型表现出不同的防御效果，但核心结论一致：所有水印均可通过合理努力被移除。

Conclusion: 现有LLM水印系统存在安全缺陷，需要改进构建方式以提升安全性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the recent state-of-the-art schemes for watermarking large language models (LLMs) outputs. These techniques are claimed to be robust, scalable and production-grade, aimed at promoting responsible usage of LLMs. We analyse the effectiveness of these watermarking techniques against an extensive collection of modified text attacks, which perform targeted semantic changes without altering the general meaning of the text content. Our approach encompasses multiple attack strategies, which include lexical alterations, machine translation, and even neural paraphrasing. The attack efficacy is measured with two target criteria - successful removal of the watermark and preservation of semantic content. We evaluate semantic preservation through BERT scores, text complexity measures, grammatical errors, and Flesch Reading Ease indices. The experimental results reveal varying levels of effectiveness among different watermarking models, with the same underlying result that it is possible to remove the watermark with reasonable effort. This study sheds light on the strengths and weaknesses of existing LLM watermarking systems, suggesting how they should be constructed to improve security of available schemes.

</details>


### [159] [Spying Across Chiplets: Side-Channel Attacks in 2.5/3D Integrated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07486)
*Giorgio Di Natale,Christelle Rabache,Pierre-Louis Hellier,Florence Podevin,Sylvain Bourdel,Romain Siragusa,Paolo Maistri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了先进封装和芯粒集成系统中的跨芯粒侧信道攻击，发现通信导向芯粒（如天线、RFID接口）可被重新用作内部观测平台，通过捕获邻近受害者芯粒的活动信号实现信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进封装和芯粒集成技术被广泛用于构建超越单片缩放极限的复杂异构系统，这些架构在带来模块化、良率和性能优势的同时，也引入了新的物理攻击面。本文旨在探索跨芯粒侧信道攻击的可行性。

Method: 提出了一种利用通信导向芯粒（如天线、RFID元件或无接触耦合结构）重新用作内部观测平台的攻击方法。通过建立现实攻击者模型，形式化描述威胁，并通过实验评估其可行性。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过这种通信导向接口捕获的信号能够揭示与邻近受害者芯粒活动相关的信息，证实了跨芯粒侧信道攻击的可行性。

Conclusion: 先进封装和芯粒集成系统面临新的侧信道攻击威胁，原本用于与外部环境交互的通信接口可被恶意重用来观测内部其他芯粒的活动，需在系统设计中考虑此类安全风险。

Abstract: Advanced packaging and chiplet-based integration are increasingly adopted to build complex heterogeneous systems beyond the limits of monolithic scaling. While these architectures offer major benefits in terms of modularity, yield, and performance, they also introduce new physical attack surfaces. In this paper, we show that side-channel attacks can be mounted across chiplets within the same package or stack. Our key idea is that a communication-oriented chiplet, originally intended to interact with the external environment through an antenna, an RFID-like element, or another contactless coupling structure, can be repurposed as an internal observation platform. We formalize this threat through a realistic adversary model, describe the corresponding attack principle, and experimentally assess its feasibility. The obtained results demonstrate that signals captured through such a communication-oriented interface can reveal information correlated with the activity of a neighboring victim chiplet.

</details>


### [160] [Cross-Modal Backdoors in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07490)
*Runhe Wang,Li Bai,Haibo Hu,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中轻量级连接器（connector）的跨模态后门攻击。仅需使用单一模态的少量样本投毒连接器，即可在另一模态的输入上激活后门，攻击成功率达99.9%（同模态）和95%以上（跨模态），且具有高度隐蔽性，现有防御手段难以有效缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要关注编码器或大语言模型等骨干组件的投毒攻击，而轻量级连接器的安全风险尚未被探索。随着开发者通过组装预训练组件构建MLLM，供应链攻击面随之增加，因此亟需研究连接器的潜在漏洞。

Method: 1）投毒连接器，使其将紧凑的潜在区域与恶意目标输出关联；2）从投毒后的潜在表示中提取恶意质心（malicious centroid）；3）通过输入侧优化（input-side optimization）将输入引导至该潜在锚点，无需重复API查询或完整模型访问。

Result: 在PandaGPT和NExT-GPT等代表性MLLM架构上，同模态设置下攻击成功率高达99.9%，跨模态设置下大多超过95.0%。该攻击保持高度隐蔽性，干净输入上泄露可忽略，且与良性连接器的权重余弦相似度保持在0.97以上。现有防御策略无法在不显著降低效用的情况下有效缓解该威胁。

Conclusion: 揭示了多模态对齐中的一个基本漏洞：单个被投毒连接器可建立可复用的跨模态潜在空间后门路径，强调了更安全的模块化MLLM设计的必要性。

Abstract: Developers increasingly construct multimodal large language models (MLLMs) by assembling pretrained components,introducing supply-chain attack surfaces.Existing security research primarily focuses on poisoning backbones such as encoders or large language models (LLMs),while the security risks of lightweight connectors remain unexplored.In this work,we propose a novel cross-modal backdoor attack that exploits this overlooked vulnerability.By poisoning only the connector using a single seed sample and several augmented variants from one modality,the adversary can subsequently activate the backdoor using inputs from other modalities.To achieve this,we first poison the connector to associate a compact latent region with a malicious target output.To activate the backdoor from other modalities,we further extract a malicious centroid from the poisoned latent representations and perform input-side optimization to steer inputs toward this latent anchor,without requiring repeated API queries or full-model access.Extensive evaluations on representative connector-based MLLM architectures,including PandaGPT and NExT-GPT,demonstrate both the effectiveness and cross-modal transferability of the proposed attack.The attack achieves up to 99.9% attack success rate (ASR) in same-modality settings,while most cross-modal settings exceed 95.0% ASR under bounded perturbations.Moreover,the attack remains highly stealthy,producing negligible leakage on clean inputs,and maintaining weight-cosine similarity above 0.97 relative to benign connectors.We further show that existing defense strategies fail to effectively mitigate this threat without incurring substantial utility degradation.These findings reveal a fundamental vulnerability in multimodal alignment: a single compromised connector can establish a reusable latent-space backdoor pathway across modalities,highlighting the need for safer modular MLLM design.

</details>


### [161] [CCX: Enabling Unmodified Intel SGX Applications on Arm CCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07548)
*Matti Schulze,Thorsten Holz,Felix Freiling*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CCX框架，能够在无需修改源代码的情况下，将现有的Intel SGX应用迁移到Arm CCA平台上运行，通过重新设计SGX功能并与Arm CCA固件集成，实现了跨架构的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Arm CPU在云环境中的广泛采用，现有的SGX应用面临严重的可移植性挑战——它们与SGX特定的API和执行语义紧密耦合，难以迁移到新兴的Arm CCA机密计算架构上。

Method: CCX在Arm CCA固件中重新设计SGX功能，将SGX抽象适配到CCA架构设计，保持与原始SGX应用的全兼容，无需修改源代码。

Result: 在QEMU模拟器和Nitrogen8M开发板上的原型评估表明，CCX能够执行现有SGX应用且无需源码修改，在评估设置中实现了性能提升，同时提供与Intel SGX相当的安全保障。

Conclusion: CCX成功解决了SGX应用向Arm CCA迁移的可移植性问题，为跨架构机密计算应用提供了可行的兼容方案。

Abstract: Novel confidential computing technologies such as Intel TDX, AMD SEV, and Arm CCA have recently emerged. In practice, due to its minimal trust boundaries, Intel SGX still remains widely used for enclave-based applications in cloud environments, including confidential cloud services, privacy-preserving communication, secure payment processing, and privacy-focused advertising. With the growing adoption of Arm CPUs in cloud systems, however, existing SGX applications face a significant portability challenge: they are tightly coupled to SGX-specific APIs and execution semantics.
  In this paper, we present the design and implementation of CCX, a framework that enables existing SGX applications to run on Arm CCA without source code modification. To this end, CCX redesigns SGX functionality within Arm CCA firmware, adapting SGX abstractions to CCA's architecture design while preserving full compatibility with existing applications originally developed for SGX. We implemented a prototype of CCX on both the QEMU emulator and a Nitrogen8M development board. Our evaluation shows that CCX is capable of executing existing SGX applications without requiring source code changes, while providing security guarantees comparable to Intel SGX and achieving performance improvements in our evaluated settings.

</details>


### [162] [GRASP -- Graph-Based Anomaly Detection Through Self-Supervised Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07812)
*Robin Buchta,Carsten Kleiner,Felix Heine,Gabi Dreo Rodosek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GRASP是一种基于掩码自监督分类的溯源入侵检测系统，通过掩码进程可执行文件信息并从其二跳溯源图邻域中推断该信息，将误分类进程标记为异常，无需阈值设定，在DARPA TC和OpTC数据集上优于现有系统，能检测所有已记录攻击及未知恶意行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于溯源的入侵检测系统（PIDS）依赖预定义或子集确定的阈值，检测稳定性受限，且忽略了进程可执行文件在描述系统活动中的关键作用。

Method: 提出GRASP，采用掩码自监督分类方法，掩码进程的可执行文件信息，学习从其二跳溯源图邻域中推断该信息，将分类错误的进程标记为异常。

Result: 在DARPA TC和OpTC数据集上，GRASP持续检测到异常行为（包括已知攻击活动），性能优于现有系统；在可执行文件行为可学习的数据集上检测出所有已记录攻击，并发现了未标记的可疑恶意行为。

Conclusion: GRASP通过无阈值、基于可执行文件行为模式学习的机制，提高了APT检测的鲁棒性和泛化能力，能有效发现已知和未知威胁。

Abstract: Advanced persistent threat (APT) attacks remain difficult to detect due to their stealth, adaptability, and use of legitimate system components. Provenance-based intrusion detection systems (PIDS) offer a promising defense by capturing detailed relationships between system components and actions. However, current PIDS rely on predefined or subset-determined thresholds, which limit detection stability and the ability to detect any anomalous behavior in general. Furthermore, related work often neglects the role of process executables, which describe system activity by interacting through a process with files, network components, and other processes. We introduce GRASP, a PIDS based on masked self-supervised classification. GRASP masks the executable information of processes and learns to infer it from their two-hop provenance graph neighborhood, marking misclassified processes as anomalies. It captures behavior patterns for the learned executables without thresholding, making it robust against interference and unknown activities. Evaluations on the DARPA TC and OpTC datasets demonstrate that GRASP consistently detects anomalous behavior, including known attack-related activities, outperforming existing systems. Our PIDS identifies all documented attacks on datasets where the behavior of executables is learnable. In addition, compared to existing systems, GRASP uncovers potentially malicious anomalous behavior not labeled as an attack in the documentation.

</details>


### [163] [Can I Check What I Designed? Mapping Security Design DSLs to Code Analyzers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07814)
*Sven Peldszus,Frederik Reiche,Kevin Hermann,Sophie Corallo,Thorsten Berger,Robert Heinrich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对66个设计级安全DSL和559个代码级安全检查进行了系统研究，发现设计与实现之间存在显著抽象鸿沟，安全专家也难以完全理解两者关系，并提出了SecLan模型作为弥合这一鸿沟的第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码级漏洞（如自动化分析工具发现的）需要在系统安全设计的上下文中评估其潜在影响，但安全设计与实现之间存在抽象鸿沟，即使是安全专家也缺乏对两者关系的完整理解。

Method: 研究了66个设计级安全DSL和36个代码级分析器的559个安全检查，识别共同概念并构建SecLan模型（经22位安全专家验证），随后通过定量分析和与9位安全专家的定性探索来研究DSL与分析器之间的关系。

Result: 设计与实现级安全之间共同点很少；安全检查常被过度通用的弱点描述，导致DSL与分析器之间存在许多非显而易见的潜在关系；安全专家也被这种复杂性所压倒。

Conclusion: 为实践者和研究者理解安全设计与实现之间的鸿沟提供了实证基础，并作为弥合这一鸿沟的第一步。

Abstract: When assessing the potential impact of code-level vulnerabilities, e.g., discovered by automated analyzers, it is essential to consider them in the context of the system's security design. However, this is a challenging task due to the abstraction gap between security design, often specified using security DSLs, and implementation. As we will show, even security experts lack a complete understanding of this relationship. Intrigued by this gap (and the general disconnect between secure design and secure implementation) we present a study of 66 design-level security DSLs and 559 security checks from 36 code-level analyzers. We identify what concepts are common to both and capture them in the SecLan model, which has been validated by 22 security experts. Based on this, we investigate the relationship between DSLs and analyzers quantitatively and explore it qualitatively together with 9 security experts. We learn that there are few commonalities between design-level and implementation-level security; security checks are often described by overly general weaknesses, resulting in many non-obvious potential relationships between security DSLs and analyzers; and even security experts are overwhelmed by this complexity. We provide an empirical basis that helps practitioners and researchers better understand the gap and serves as a first step toward bridging it.

</details>


### [164] [Longitudinal Analyses of SAST Tools: A CodeQL Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07900)
*Jean-Charles Noirot Ferrand,Kyle Domico,Yohan Beugin,Patrick McDaniel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对114个CodeQL版本、3993个CVE和1622个仓库的纵向分析（累计分析超200亿行代码），评估了SAST工具的有效性、可操作性和稳定性。CodeQL检测到171个CVE，其中83个可在修复前被检测到；50%的检测中，漏洞文件中超过50%的发现位于漏洞位置；但CVE检测并非单调递增，21个CVE因版本更新而丢失检测，17个从未被重新检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件流水线广泛使用自动化静态分析工具来防止漏洞引入，但目前缺乏对这些工具在整个开源生态系统中长期有效性的理解。

Method: 提出一种通过纵向测量评估SAST工具的新方法，对CodeQL的114个历史版本在1622个仓库的3993个CVE上进行分析，累计分析超过200亿行代码，从有效性、可操作性和稳定性三个维度进行评估。

Result: CodeQL共检测到171个CVE，其中83个可在修复前被检测到；50%的检测中漏洞文件内超过50%的发现位于漏洞位置；21个CVE因版本更新丢失检测，17个从未被重新检测到。

Conclusion: 使用SAST工具是防范漏洞引入的最佳实践，但开发者应警惕工具版本更新可能带来的检测盲区。

Abstract: Open-source software (OSS) pipelines rely on automated static analysis tools to prevent the introduction of vulnerabilities in code. However, there is limited understanding of the efficacy of these tools across the OSS ecosystem over time. In this paper, we introduce a novel method to evaluate static application security testing (SAST) tools through longitudinal measurements and perform the largest academic study of CodeQL -- the most prevalent static analysis tool from GitHub -- on OSS codebases. We apply our apparatus on 114 versions of CodeQL over time on 3993 CVEs from 1622 repositories to measure key properties of the tool, culminating in more than 20 billion lines of code analyzed. First, we measure its effectiveness, i.e., its ability to detect vulnerabilities before they are fixed. Then, we determine whether these detections were actionable through two measures of the distance between findings and vulnerability location either over the entire codebase or within the vulnerable file. Finally, we study the stability of CodeQL by examining how vulnerability detections hold across versions and the evolution of CodeQL on the accuracy-precision trade-off. We find that CodeQL identifies a total of 171 CVEs, and that for 83 of them, a CodeQL version prior to the fix could detect it. Such detections are in general actionable if findings are triaged across files, as for 50% of the 171 detections, more than 50% of findings in the vulnerable file are located in the vulnerable location. Finally, we show that CVE detections are not monotonic across versions as 21 CVEs were no longer detected following a version change and 17 that were never redetected. Our study shows that using SAST tools is a matter of best practice as they prevent numerous vulnerabilities from being introduced, but that developers should be aware of changes that may leave blind spots in detections upon updates of the tool.

</details>
